Problem! There are **two** dreamer processes for 008e v2 running simultaneously:
1. PID 124960 (child of 124959, started at 12:26) - this is from the old tmux session that I killed earlier, but the process itself survived
2. PID 125397 (child of 125396/125392, started at 12:27) - the one I just submitted

Both are writing to the same logdir `exp_008e_v2_subsample_on_dt1_h15`. This is exactly the contamination problem we're trying to fix!


I need to kill the old process (PID 124960 and its parent 124959) while keeping the new one. Both are claiming GPU 1 with CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1, though nvidia-smi only shows 3MiB usage there—maybe neither has loaded models yet or one crashed. The older process has been running for about 6.5 minutes at 99% CPU while the newer one is at 2.5 minutes, also maxing out CPU.

Neither process is showing up on the GPU yet. The old process came from a tmux session I killed, and even though it should have received SIGHUP and exited via its trap handler, it's still running. I need to forcefully kill PID 124960 and trace back through its parent chain (124959, 124955) since they're all from the contaminated old session. I also notice the new logdir was already renamed to `exp_008e_v2_dead_20260514_1227`, but I need to verify what's happening with both processes.

The old process still has the logdir path in its command line, and since it was already running when I renamed the directory, it likely still has file handles pointing to the renamed location. When the new process started, it would have created a fresh directory with the same name. The old process might have the directory name cached or still be writing to the renamed directory through its existing file handles.

The safest approach is to kill the old process and its parent processes so only the new one continues running. I should also verify when the new process started and what it's currently doing.

2. Kill the tmux session
3. Rename the contaminated v2 directory
4. Start a completely fresh v3

Let me do it properly this time: kill the process FIRST, then kill the tmux session, then rename the directory, then submit fresh.

- GPU 0: 008a (106947) and 008d (109524) running normally

The dreamer prefill phase (random episode collection) is CPU-bound and doesn't touch the GPU until the model is initialized for training. For this configuration with 4 random episodes, it should take a few minutes. The process is healthy.


The exp_id got bumped from v2 to v3 after we cleared out the v2 contamination.
